| KEY-STRAIGHTEN | Straighten while held — ⌘ drag | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:crop.straighten`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/detail.rs` (`straighten_overlay`) | ⌘-drag in Crop draws a level line; the crop tool stays active |
| KEY-SLIDER-RESET | Reset slider — double-click | P0 | ✅ | `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_slider_reset.rs` | double-click a slider's name or thumb: back to its default as one undo step (a click on the thumb no longer moves it first, which made it two); resting the pointer on either says so (“Exposure — double-click to reset it”, as the curve channels), the value keeps “Click to type a value” (issue #1436); a disabled slider's name doesn't reset it. No per-slider reset button (Lightroom has none); no ⌥ panel headings turning into Reset buttons yet (`cmd:develop.resetSection` is command-only) |
| KEY-SLIDER-NUDGE | Nudge slider — ↑/↓ | P1 | ✅ | `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets.rs` (`nudged`), `cmd:develop.adjust` | ↑/↓ over any slider: ≈ 1/200 of its range (exposure 0.05, most sliders 1, temperature 50 K); ⇧ ×5; one undo step each. Scrolling sideways over any slider (trackpad swipe, tilting wheel, ⇧+wheel) nudges it the same way, one wheel notch a step, without selecting it first; only a mostly sideways movement counts, so scrolling the panel leaves sliders alone (`crates/ui-egui/src/tests_masking.rs`, `sideways_scrolling_adjusts_the_slider_under_the_pointer`). The vertical wheel nudges only a slider a click or drag selected, while the pointer stays in its row: leaving the row (even within one frame) disarms it until the next click or drag, and Tab focus arms only the arrow keys (issue #1355, `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets/tests_numeric_entry.rs`) |
| KEY-SLIDER-TYPE | Type a slider value | P1 | ✅ | `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets.rs` (`typed_value`), `crates/ui-egui/src/text_field.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets/tests_numeric_entry.rs` (`opening_release_honors_return_and_escape_immediately`, `a_long_click_keeps_the_field_open`, `dragging_the_value_sideways_scrubs_it`), `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_keymap.rs` (`opening_numeric_release_keeps_keys_in_the_editor`) | click the value next to any slider's name and type one (`1.5`, `+0,25`, `-20`, `5600 K`): Return or clicking away applies it on the control's steps and range as one undo step, Esc keeps the old value; issue #322; Return/Escape take effect on the opening release, retaining the stored value on Escape; typing stays in the editor without triggering global shortcuts; the field stays open however long the click is held, and a sideways drag on the value scrubs it (fork #59) |
| KEY-SLIDER-SCRUB | Scrub a slider value — drag the number | P1 | ✅ | `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets.rs` (`slider`, `SCRUB_START`), `crates/ui-egui/src/widgets/tests_numeric_entry.rs` (`dragging_the_value_sideways_scrubs_it_like_the_thumb`, `scrubbing_back_and_with_shift`, `a_press_that_barely_moves_still_types`) | drag the value next to any slider's name sideways: it moves as dragging the thumb would (the pointer's travel over the track's width, ⇧ a tenth as far), on the control's steps and range, as one undo step; a press that moves less than 3 points sideways stays a click that types a value (KEY-SLIDER-TYPE); the pointer over the value is the horizontal-resize cursor, as Lightroom's scrubby value |

## Lightroom Classic extras

Classic-only features and Classic keys (grouped), described in `plan/lightroom-classic/`. Rows added on 2026-10-10 from
PLAN.md §2 carry the status of that survey ("unverified" where it said so) until someone checks them.

| Id | Feature | Tier | Status | Evidence | Missing / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LRC-LIB-IMPORT | Full import dialog | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:library.import` (`mode`, `destination`, `organize`, `rename`, `metadataPreset`, `preset`, `keywords`, `album`), `crates/ui-egui/src/import.rs` | review grid with duplicates skipped; add in place or copy (library Originals or any folder; by day / by month / one folder / custom folder template such as `{date:%Y}/{date:%Y%m%d}` → 2026/20260114 or `{date:%Y}/{date:%B}` → 2026/January, kept inside the destination; example destination shown; rename template with live example, Tags picker (insert at cursor) and unknown-tag warning, numbered across the import); develop preset, metadata preset, keywords, album on import |
| LRC-LIB-AUTOIMPORT | Watched-folder import | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:library.autoImport` | see LR-IMP-AUTO |
| LRC-LIB-TETHER | Tethered capture | P2 | 🟡 | `cmd:tether.start` | Studio capture: watched folder, import with session, naming, develop/metadata presets, keywords and a collection; the newest shot opens in the loupe. Native PTP (`cmd:tether.connect`, `cmd:tether.capture`, `crates/tether/src/ptp/`): pure-Rust PTP codec, USB (nusb) and PTP/IP transports, a simulated camera for tests; capture, object-added events → download into the session folder → the usual import; the connection holds an activity-stack row whose ✕ disconnects. File ▸ Tethered Capture sits after Import from Device and opens with Start Tethered Capture… (issue #120; it used to land at the bottom of File). Not yet verified on a real camera body |
| LRC-LIB-VIEWS | Grid / loupe / compare / survey / people | P1 | 🟡 | `cmd:view.photoGrid`, `cmd:view.squareGrid`, `cmd:view.detail`, `cmd:view.compare`, `cmd:view.survey`, `cmd:view.gridInfo` | grid, square grid, loupe, compare, survey, second window; square-grid captions: file name, exposure or capture date (View ▸ Grid View Style; picking one in Photo Grid switches to Square Grid); no people view |
| LRC-LIB-COMPARE | Compare view | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:view.compare` | ⇧C (C is Crop here); swap, make select; a filmstrip click replaces the active side, a ⌘/Ctrl- or Shift-click changes the selection without touching the pair; zoom always linked |
| LRC-LIB-SURVEY | Survey view | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:view.survey` | N |
| LRC-LIB-REFVIEW | Reference view | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:view.reference`, `cmd:photo.setReference`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/compare.rs` (`show_reference`) | View ▸ Before/After ▸ Reference View (⇧R): the reference photo beside the active one (edited with the Edit panel); photo menu ▸ Set as Reference Photo |
| LRC-LIB-CATALOG-PANEL | Catalog sets | P1 | 🟡 | `cmd:library.source` (`missing`, `previousImport`, `previousExport`, `quickCollection`), `cmd:library.showQuickCollection`, `cmd:album.clearQuick`, `cmd:album.saveQuick` | all, Quick Collection (with count; ⌘B shows it, ⌥⌘B clears it, ⌘⇧B saves it as an album and clears it, one undo step), previous import (the latest import batch), recently added, picks, recently deleted, missing photos (when any), previous export (`previousExport`: the photos of the last export) |
| LRC-LIB-CATALOG-IMPORT | Native Lightroom Classic catalog import | P1 | 🟡 | `cmd:library.inspectLightroom`, `cmd:library.importLightroom`, `cmd:file.importLightroom`, `crates/engine/src/lightroom_catalog.rs`, `crates/engine/src/capture_one_catalog.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/lightroom_destination.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_lightroom_destination.rs`, `docs/lightroom-catalog-import.md` | before the import starts, File ▸ Import Lightroom Catalog… asks where the catalog goes: merge into the open library (named, with its photo count; preselected when it is empty) or create a new library (named after the catalog file, next to the open library, both editable), which is made and opened following File ▸ New Catalog…'s rules before the import; Cancel does nothing; Settings ▸ General ▸ "When importing a Lightroom catalog" (Ask where to import it / Always merge into the open library) or the window's "Don't ask again" turns it off; `file.importLightroom` takes `into` (`open` / `new`), `name`, `location` to choose without the window; a session in memory merges without asking (issue #2672); read-only `.lrcat` plus committed WAL pages (also WAL-only tables with main schema format 0), in-tree read-only Rust SQLite B-tree reader; MAIN pages are read from the file on demand with no whole-file size cap or allocation, so catalogs over 1 GiB import (upstream #798/#1819); the committed WAL is streamed into a compact page index (1 GiB, 4,000,000 frames); MAIN is bounded to 16,777,216 pages, each B-tree walk to 4,000,000 pages, records to 64 MiB, XMP packets to 16 MiB; close Lightroom and all catalog writers first (metadata/header/companion checks are best-effort, not a transactional snapshot, and a source that fails or changes mid-read fails the import); background inspection/import with progress/cancellation, per-open library identity checks; persistent result with destination library, offline-original counts and Show in Library, dismissed when switching libraries; offline Local records are promoted without probing missing files; originals in place, ratings/flags/labels, XMP, hierarchical keywords, collection sets/regular collections, saved prints as Saved Prints (page, layout style, Custom Package cells with their photos, image, page and Print Job settings; reimport upgrades an earlier plain album once), smart collections as smart albums when every rule has an equivalent (rating, pick, label, edited, ISO/focal length/aperture, keyword/filename/camera/lens/title/caption/location text rules, capture / edit date rules (incl. today, yesterday, this week / month / year), collection-name rules resolved to the imported albums, nested all/any/none groups; otherwise current membership with the reason reported), folder stacks (order and collapsed state), virtual copies, mapped develop settings, develop history replayed as History (named steps, oldest first, unreadable steps warned), snapshots as Versions (named, unreadable ones warned), 105 of the 113 flat `crs:` fields of Adobe's public XMP docs mapped (`develop_field_coverage_over_public_crs_fields`); stable reimport identities, undo, missing-file placeholders, a root folder not found at its recorded path looked up relative to the catalog (Lightroom's `relativePathFromCatalog`, so a project folder copied from another computer stays linked; reported as a warning), existing edits preserved. Best-effort deduplicated recovery archives retain settings/history/snapshots/collection rules (32 MiB each, eight archives/128 MiB aggregate); the large develop, history, snapshot and metadata tables are streamed row by row with no row or payload cap (issue #1234), tables kept whole are capped at 1,000,000 rows and 256 MiB; develop settings that can't be read fail the import with a clear error rather than importing every photo unedited; archive limits/write failures and unreadable optional tables (history, snapshots, metadata) warn without blocking photo import; unrelated unsupported tables and opaque SQL BLOBs skipped, except the compressed history-step and snapshot settings of Lightroom Classic 13 and later, which are decoded (they import as History and Versions and are kept in the archive; an archive too big with them is kept without them). Custom white balance on relative-WB raws (`RAW` in Lightroom's terms) is shifted from the as-shot white in the photo's history (read row by row; upstream #3906), and stays As Shot and is reported when the history has none (issue #510). Adobe rendering, AI data and metadata-status smart rules remain incomplete; no writes to Lightroom catalogs. Open Catalog given a Lightroom catalog folder, an `.lrdata` / `.lrcat-data` folder or the `.lrcat` makes no library there and offers this import with the catalog found (`cmd:app.openLibrary`, `crates/engine/src/lightroom_folder.rs`; issue #891); an image record without its file, folder or root record is left out with a warning instead of failing the import; records Lightroom marks `VIDEO` import as videos (and a re-import repairs an earlier one as one undo step). The same command reads a Capture One catalog (`.cocatalogdb`, C1-IMPORT; checked on Capture One 16.9.0): photos, variants as virtual copies, star ratings, colour tags (orange and pink without a label, warned), IPTC fields, hierarchical keywords, albums inside their projects and groups (Recent Imports, All Images and the trash left out), and for offline originals the catalog's capture time, camera and lens; the menu item, file dialog (a filter per catalog kind), windows, messages and undo step name the program; Capture One develop settings (`crates/engine/src/capture_one_develop.rs`, C1-DEVELOP): exposure, orientation and straighten, crop and aspect, flips, white balance as the change from as shot, checked against Capture One's exports with `<cli> compare`; every other setting a photo uses is named per tool in the result window; not yet: the other develop settings (Brightness, Levels, HDR, curves, detail, colour, lens, LCC, spots, local layers) and smart albums (warned) |
| LRC-LIB-CATALOG-UPGRADE | Older catalogs upgraded with a kept copy | P1 | ✅ | `crates/catalog/src/upgrade_backup.rs`, `crates/catalog/src/tests_upgrade_backup.rs`, `crates/engine/src/tests_library.rs`, `cmd:library.info` | before a catalog format upgrade the library is copied to `backups/before-v<N> <time>/` and verified byte for byte (the older version opens that folder), then upgraded in place; a full disk, a crash or a damaged catalog mid-upgrade leaves the library as the older version left it with an error that says what to do; the app tells once what was upgraded, where the copy is and how to go back (idea from OneSlips' upgraded _V10 copy) |
| LRC-LIB-CATALOG-LOCK | Catalog open in one program at a time | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:app.openLibrary`, `crates/catalog/src/lock.rs`, `crates/engine/src/library.rs` (`open_library`) | exclusive OS lock on `catalog.lock` for the session (app and `app-cli --library`); a second opener is refused with who has it (`catalog.lock.owner`: program, process, computer); released by the OS on a crash, so never stale; file systems without locks open unprotected with a warning (the app's notices, `library.info` → `settingsWarnings`, and the CLI's stderr; issue #171); no read-only mode; the browser build (OPFS) has no guard across tabs yet. A library that can't be opened at launch (locked, unreadable, newer format, drive missing) shows a window — Try Again, Choose Another Catalog…, Continue Without Saving (then a banner; nothing written), Quit — instead of a silent demo session (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/library_problem.rs`); the lock is released explicitly when the library closes, not by letting its file handle close, so a program another thread is starting at that moment (which holds a copy of every file handle until it is launched) cannot keep the library "already open" for a moment (issue #680) |
| LRC-LIB-CATALOG-SWITCH | New / Open Recent catalog, catalog name shown | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:app.newLibrary`, `cmd:app.openLibrary`, `cmd:app.recentLibraries`, `cmd:app.clearRecentLibraries`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/settings.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/left.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/library_problem.rs` (`launch_plan`, `show_chooser`), `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_launch_library.rs` | File ▸ Libraries ▸ New Catalog… (name, then location), File ▸ Libraries (last 8; missing ones greyed out, dropped when opened; Remove from List ▸ one entry, or `app.recentLibraries {remove}`, which forgets only the path; Clear), the open library's name under My Photos and in the window title (issue #902); a library that can't be opened leaves the current one open; ⌘O (Ctrl+O on Windows and Linux) runs Open Catalog… and asks for a folder once (`crates/ui-egui/src/tests_keymap.rs`: `cmd_o_opens_the_library_picker_once`); a dialog or a text field with the focus keeps the key; Settings ▸ General ▸ At launch: Open the last library (default) or Ask me, a window listing the recent libraries (name, folder, when last opened; missing, unreadable or no-longer-library ones greyed out with the reason, checked read-only — nothing is locked or written, so a library another program has open is reported, with who has it, when Open is pressed; the last one selected; Open, Open Another…, New Catalog…, Remove from List, Quit; ↑ ↓ Return Esc), also shown once when Alt/Option is held in the first 2 s after the window appears (macOS reports an Option key held since before launch only once it changes); `--library` and `<PREFIX>_LIBRARY` come first and `lcc-cli` never asks (issue #1290) |
| LRC-LIB-FOLDERS | Disk folder tree | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:library.browse`, `cmd:folder.rename`, `cmd:folder.move`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/left.rs` (`local_section`, `folder_tree`) | standard places + the browsed folder + Browse Folder…, each an expandable tree of subfolders, breadcrumb navigation; context menu: Rename Folder…, Move Folder To…, Show in Finder (on disk, photos relinked; one undo step each: undo / redo move the folder back and forth, refused when the destination is taken) |
| LRC-LIB-LIBFOLDERS | Library folders | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:library.folders`, `cmd:library.source` (`libraryFolder`), `cmd:library.filter` (`libraryFolder`), `cmd:library.removeFolder`, `cmd:library.showSubfolderPhotos`, `cmd:folder.rename`, `cmd:folder.move`, `cmd:folder.label`, `cmd:folder.create`, `cmd:photo.moveToFolder`, `cmd:folder.scanChanges`, `cmd:folder.synchronize`, `crates/catalog/src/folders.rs`, `crates/engine/src/sync.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/sync.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/left.rs` (`folders_section`, `folder_menu_for_library`, `row_menu`), `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_panels.rs`, `cmd:library.volumes`, `cmd:folder.create`, `cmd:folder.delete`, `cmd:folder.sync`, `cmd:folder.relocate`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/folders.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/folders.rs` | sidebar Folders: the folders the library's photos were imported from, with photo counts (subfolders included), as another way to explore the library next to By Date and Keywords; Local stays the way to browse any folder on disk. Each disk is a row of its own and starts open: a drive letter, a Windows share (`\\server\share`, `\\?\UNC\…`, `\\wsl$\…`), a macOS share or disk (`/Volumes/tokyo`), a Linux mount (`/media`, `/run/media`, `/mnt`, gvfs `smb-share:…`, `/net/<host>`), NFS (`/Network/Servers/<host>`), else “This Computer” — told apart from the stored paths alone, so an offline share lists like a mounted one and the sidebar never waits on a network. Only folders that hold imported photos are listed (browsed, deleted and demo photos don't count); every folder is a row of its own (`Users` → `me` → `Pictures`), and a folder that holds nothing itself and leads to one folder starts open; every level indents one step more than its parent however deep it goes: the whole sidebar (Albums, Local, By Date, Folders, Keywords) scrolls sideways when its widest row is wider than the panel (rows are measured one frame behind; Create Album and the counts stay at the visible edge); a name that does not fit ends in an ellipsis at the count (the full name on hover; a row asks for room for at most 140 pt of its name, so depth, not one long name, makes the sidebar scroll), as does the grid title; selection and hover bars end at the panel's edge; click makes the folder the source, like an album or a Local folder (its photos and those of the folders inside it fill the grid — Show Photos in Subfolders, in the header's + menu and every folder's menu, turned off shows the folder's own photos only, kept with the library's view —, titled “photos/travel”; the filter bar narrows it further; `library.source` `libraryFolder` + `path`; kept with the library's view), and its row is always opened into view; the shown folder follows a rename or move and their undo (engine-tested; the sidebar test covers rename), is dropped back to All Photos when it is removed or no longer holds a photo, and a smart album made from the view keeps it, also when its rules are edited (the editor has no folder field). Only a folder that shows exactly its own photos can be shown (not the startup disk, not `/Volumes`-like folders above disks, not an empty one). A row that would cover other disks' photos too (the startup disk, `/Volumes`, `/mnt`…) only opens. Right-click (row or triangle): Rename Folder…, Move Folder To…, New Folder on Disk… (`folder.create`; one undo step, undo removes it while empty), Move Selected Photos Here (`photo.moveToFolder`, also *Move to Folder…* in the photo context menu, and photos dragged from the grid onto a Folders row: the row lights up, the dragged label says “Move 3 photos to “Trip””, a closed folder held under the pointer opens, a row covering several disks and Local photos are refused with the reason, photos already there say so; a drop asks first (“Move 2 photos to “Porto”?”, Don't ask again, Settings → Confirm before moving photos to another folder on disk), and photos that leave the view leave the selection; files and XMP sidecars move, virtual copies follow, across volumes copied (streamed) and verified before the source goes, a name taken in the destination refuses the whole move, one undo step; an undo with the share offline or read-only is refused with the reason and kept until it can run, nothing deleted), Show in Finder (on disk, photos relinked, as in Local), Set Color Label ▸ (the five colours with their custom names, None, Edit Label Names…; `folder.label`, one undo step; the row shows the label's dot before its count and says it with its name; kept in the catalog as a folder record under the folder's identity, so it follows Rename / Move / Update Folder Location and their undo and redo, the folders inside going along), Synchronize Folder… (the dialog opens at once and scans the folder and the folders inside it on a worker thread — the look for new files beside parallel checks of the library's photos — then offers Import new photos (checked; files only browsed in Local count as new), Relink photos whose file was renamed or moved within the folder (checked; matched by content — a Duplicate's too — or, for a photo with no hash on record such as one from a Lightroom catalog, by file size and dimensions when exactly one file agrees), Remove missing photos from the library (to Recently Deleted) and Read metadata updates from XMP sidecars (saved after the photo came in or was last edited here, and saying something the library doesn't; the sidecar wins), with counts and skipped duplicates / unreadable files; `folder.scanChanges` / `folder.synchronize`, which acts on exactly the scan shown — a scan whose folder's photos changed meanwhile is refused and made again in the background — and is one undo step, also with a full history; a folder that isn't there (an unplugged disk) is reported as such, not as every photo missing; a whole disk or a folder holding disks needs `disk: true`; Esc stops the scan; Synchronize hands everything to a worker thread — the import, the checks that moved and missing files are still as found, the sidecar reads — and commits its steps between frames under a progress window with Cancel, so no file is touched on the UI thread) and Remove from Catalog… (asks; moves the folder's photos to Recently Deleted, one undo step, no file touched); a disk row (not the startup disk) offers Set Color Label ▸, Synchronize Folder… and Remove Disk from Catalog… (the command needs `disk: true` for a whole disk or a folder spanning several). Limits: names are compared as written (macOS `/Volumes/tokyo` vs `/Volumes/TOKYO` are two disks; `/Volumes/Macintosh HD/…` and `/…` two rows); no disk capacity / online status; photos can't yet be dragged onto a folder row (use the menus); a name clash on moving photos is refused rather than renamed -1, -2; folders can't be filtered or sorted by label, and a label stays with the old path when a folder moved outside the app is relinked with Find Missing (it relinks photos, not folder records; Update Folder Location, `cmd:folder.relocate`, carries the labels and leaves those of folders already at the new place); Synchronize has no “show import dialog first” or “show missing photos” option, and only knows when a photo came in or was last developed here: a rating, label or metadata change made here (auto-write off) after another app saved the sidecar is listed as an update, and reading it restores the sidecar's older value (reading is never the default); the walk for new files isn't cancelled midway and its depth/entry limits are only logged. A folder holding no imported photo is never listed: one just created on disk (New Folder on Disk…) shows up only once photos are imported into it (the reference app lists empty disk folders too — a deliberate divergence; Local is the way to work in such a folder meanwhile). P1.4 (Classic Folders panel, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/folders.rs`): volume rows with a status light and free / total space (`cmd:library.volumes`), Create Folder… / Create Folder Inside… (`cmd:folder.create`, undoable), the fork's quick Synchronize (`cmd:folder.sync`), Update Folder Location… (`cmd:folder.relocate`), Show Parent Folder, and `cmd:folder.delete` for an empty folder (undoable); a folder's context menu has one grouped order in Folders and Local (`disk_folder_menu`, `folder_menu_groups`): library photos (Add N New Photos…, Synchronize Folder…, Locate Missing Files…, Update Folder Location…), the disk (New Folder on Disk…, Rename, Move, Move Selected Photos Here), Set Color Label, Show in Finder / Show Parent Folder, then Keep in / Remove from Local or Remove from Library; a row that can't act now is grayed out with the reason instead of vanishing (upstream #2935); every folder menu has Copy Path (native separators, a bare drive as its root), a disk's row has Show in Finder and New Folder on Disk…, a Local row New Folder on Disk… (upstream #2959); a Folders folder's or disk's menu has Export Folder… and Export Folder with Preset ▸ (the folder and the folders inside it, as the row shows them; grayed out with the reason for a row covering several disks or holding no photo; upstream #2956); Import to This Folder… heads a Folders row's menu (pick a source; the review copies into this folder, Into one folder; `file.addFolder {destination}`) and Add to My Photos… a Local row's (the review, adding in place); both grayed out while an import runs (upstream #2961) |
| LRC-LIB-FOLDER-POLL | Folder polling, new-file policy, sync hand-off (issue #123) | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:folder.watchPreferences`, `cmd:folder.poll`, `cmd:folder.newFiles`, `cmd:folder.synchronize`, `crates/engine/src/folder_watch.rs`, `crates/engine/src/link.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/folder_poll.rs`, `apps/cli/src/sync_folder.rs`, `crates/engine/src/tests_folder_watch.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_folder_poll.rs` | Settings ▸ Library ▸ Folder Polling (off by default: costly on a VPN or slow link): on library open, when the app comes to the front and every 1 / 5 / 15 / 60 minutes, each folder the library holds photos in is listed once on a worker thread (no file opened) and stamped (modification time + a hash of its entry names, kept in `folder-stamps.json`); only folders whose stamp changed are scanned again with Synchronize Folder's scan; the poll is one activity row (✕ stops it). New files: Ask (default) puts “N new” on the folder's Folders row, a notice, and Add N New Photos… in its right-click menu (opens Synchronize Folder); Add automatically imports them in place in the background (Synchronize Folder's import half). Hand-off: `<cli> sync-folder [--connect ADDR | --library DIR] FOLDER` and `<cli> open-url URL` run `folder.synchronize`; the desktop app given `<scheme>://sync?path=…` (scheme from `brand.toml`) opens Synchronize Folder for it. Limits: a new folder beside the library's folders (not inside one) isn't noticed; the first poll of a library only records stamps; the URL scheme isn't registered with the OS yet, so a link reaches the app only as a launch argument or through the CLI; files still being copied and other apps' XMP are separate work |

File Path — the whole string, either separator, case-insensitive), Quick Collection and target album (B in the grids adds / removes the selection; album menu ▸ Set as Target Album; the target is marked +). P1.4: Library's Classic Collections panel (the tree with drag and drop, + menu); a “Target: …” line under the tree with × back to the Quick Collection; Export Collection Definition… (a collection, smart collection or set with all inside it, as JSON) and Import Collection Definition… (+ menu or a set's menu; photos matched by path, else file name + capture time; one undo step). Missing: Classic's “collection set” naming in the UI (sets are album folders), exporting as a catalog |
| LRC-LIB-SMARTCOLL | Smart-collection rules | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:album.createSmart`, `cmd:album.setRules`, `cmd:album.ruleFields`, `cmd:dialog.smartAlbum`, `crates/catalog/src/rules.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/rules_editor.rs` | rule editor: match all / any / none, nested groups (⌥+ or + Group), 47 fields. The field menu shows Rating, Pick Flag, Color Label and Any Searchable Text, then one submenu per group (`FIELD_GROUPS`; `album.ruleFields` reports each field's `group`): Source (album — a plain or a smart album, so one smart album can include or exclude another; a rule that would make an album include itself, directly or through others, is refused, and a loop saved anyway can't recurse: inside it, the album being evaluated counts as holding no photos, so an "isn't" rule on it matches every photo, and the sidebar marks it ⚠ —, virtual copy, copy name, in a stack, and the fork's Immich link state), File (filename, extension, file path, type, format, video duration), Date (capture / import / edit), Keywords & People (keywords, keyword count, people on named faces, people count), Description (title, caption, alt text, creator, copyright, copyright status), Camera Info (camera, lens, focal length, aperture, shutter speed as 1/250, ISO), Location (any, city, state / province, country, GPS), Size (long / short edge, aspect ratio and megapixels, all of the photo as shown: oriented and cropped), Develop (has edits, cropped, treatment), Assisted Culling (focus, best of similar shots); text / number / date / in-the-last operators; live match count; also usable as a library filter (`ruleSet`). Missing vs Classic: develop preset, flash, exposure bias, camera serial, colour profile / bit depth, IPTC Job, smart-preview status. Choice values read as words in every language (Public Domain, Black & White; `album.ruleFields` gives `choiceLabels`), while rules store the ids; yes/no fields read Yes / No and take `true`/`false`, `"yes"`/`"no"`, `"true"`/`"false"` or 1/0. Every value is checked against its field (`RuleSet::check`): numbers (rating 0–5), shutter speeds, dates (`2026`, `2026-08`, `2026-08-14`, then `T10`, `T10:00` or `T10:00:00`, a space working as the `T`, and after whole seconds a fraction and a time zone as imported capture times carry them (`.250+02:00`); no 30 February; `rules::rule_date` is the one reading for the check and the matcher; each date field has a calendar picker, `crates/ui-egui/src/date_picker.rs`, that picks a year, a month or a day and keeps a "between" in order), between = two bounds in order, in the last = more than 0 of a known unit, choices (ids, the flag's picked / rejected, a colour label's custom name), text with something to look for, an existing album (plain or smart, not a folder), no empty groups; each problem names its rule (`rule 2.1: no rating 0–5 is 9`), and album.createSmart / setRules / library.filter refuse them. The editor marks each bad rule under its row (inside groups too), keeps OK disabled until they are fixed (and its confirm refuses them before renaming anything), picks an Album rule's album with an album picker (`crates/ui-egui/src/album_picker.rs`: the sidebar's tree with folders, or type part of a name or folder; ↑ / ↓ / Enter; albums that can't be picked shown greyed with the reason) (is / isn't; a folder can't be tested), and drawing it never changes a rule: a rating of 9, "in the last 0 days" or 20,000 days, a "between" that isn't a pair all stay as stored (and are marked where they can't match) until edited. Album rules saved with an old operator (≥, between) are read as the "isn't" they always meant (`RuleSet::upgrade`). Saved smart albums whose rules no longer check are marked ⚠ in the sidebar (tooltip: what to fix; `albums.list` `problems`, `Catalog::smart_album_problems`); the name and rules of an album are saved as one undo step (`album.setRules` `name`). Problems read as the field's name and the issue in the editor's language (“Titel: braucht einen Suchbegriff”; `Problem::issue` / `field`, `Issue::text`, translated in every locale), while commands keep the English `rule 2.1: …` message for agents; rules and groups reorder from a move handle with the arrow keys (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/rules_move.rs`), no drag yet |

SFTP service (`kind: sftp`, `settings: {server: a web.saveServer name or {host, port, user, path, keyFile}}`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/publish_sftp.rs`): one folder per collection, re-publish replaces, removal deletes on the server. End-to-end gate: `apps/cli/tests/session_e2e.rs` (tether → cull → develop → Hard Drive, SFTP, Immich → sync back → contact sheet PDF). Publish panel: set up and edit Hard Drive, SFTP, Immich and plug-in services; SFTP remembers the host key on first use; Immich publish links the uploaded originals. Grid sections: a published collection lists New Photos to Publish, Modified Photos to Re-Publish and Published Photos under their own headers (`crates/engine/src/cmd/publish.rs` `sections`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/grid.rs`). Deleted Photos to Remove: published photos taken out of the collection (or deleted) are listed last under their own header. Not done: publish services beyond Hard Drive / SFTP / Immich (Flickr, SmugMug) |

The list is there without a selection too; Put New Keywords Inside This Keyword (kept with the keyword in the library, undoable) also places new keywords typed in the Keywords panel. Keyword tag options include on export, export containing keywords and export synonyms shape exported files (names in `dc:subject`, paths in `lr:hierarchicalSubject`), and files' `lr:hierarchicalSubject` paths come back as keywords on import and from sidecars; Person is stored only. Settings ▸ Interface ▸ Keywords of the selection ▸ Grouped by parent (`keywordChips: grouped` in the app settings; Whole names stays the default): keywords inside the same keyword share a line naming it (`keywordGroup:<parent>`: the deepest keyword a chip shares with another) and their chips say only what is below it, side by side; a list of more than ten lines stops after eight with “+N more” (`keywordChipsMore`, then `keywordChipsFewer`); the whole list is for the selection it was asked of, another selection's is short again, and the suggestions say the keyword's own name (its whole path when two share a name, and in the tooltip). The titles of Keywords and of the Keyword List fold their sections shut (`section:keywording`, `section:keywordList`; kept in the UI state as `foldedSections`), and a selection without keywords says “No keywords yet”. The Add keyword box offers the library's keywords for what is being typed (after the last comma) right under itself (`kwComplete:<keyword>`, at most eight, those every selected photo has left out, a keyword spelled two ways once; above the box where the window has no room under it, and not at all while the box is scrolled out of the panel): ↓ / ↑ without Shift or Alt move through them (`kwCompleteAt:<keyword>`; the highlight stays with its keyword when the library changes), Return or a click gives the highlighted one with the keywords typed before it and leaves the cursor in the box; with none highlighted Return gives what was typed, as before. A name the library doesn't have is announced as a new keyword, with the keyword it would go inside (`kwCompleteCreate`). The suggestions below no longer change while typing. A refused add says why and keeps the text. With several photos selected the box says how many its keywords are of (`keywordScope`); a chip's × is a target as tall as the chip and 22 pt wide that lights up on its own, and taking a keyword off several photos (× or the chip's menu) says which keyword, from how many photos, and that Edit ▸ Undo puts it back; a refused removal says why. Chips and their × show the keyboard focus. Edit Keyword Set (Keyword Set ▸ Edit Set…): a set's name and nine slots typed in place, empty slots kept so each keyword stays on its ⌥ key, a set renamed in place (`cmd:keyword.saveSet` `replace`) or saved as a new set (`new`, a taken name refused). File ▸ More Imports ▸ Import Keywords… / Export Keywords… (`cmd:keyword.import`, `cmd:keyword.export`): keyword list files in Lightroom Classic's format (tab-indented, `{synonym}`, `[left out of export]`), which Capture One, darktable, Bridge and Photo Supreme (its Formatted Vocabulary File) read and write too; import adds what is missing in one undo step, export names the keywords Capture One refuses (; , < >). Built-in keyword sets Outdoor / Portrait / Wedding Photography (usable, not deletable; a user set of the same name replaces one) and the keyword shortcut (Set Keyword Shortcut… `cmd:dialog.keywordShortcut` ⌘⌥⇧K takes several keywords separated by commas, `cmd:keyword.setShortcut`, `cmd:keyword.shortcut`, `cmd:keyword.toggleShortcut` ⇧K; Use This as Keyword Shortcut and a + in the Keyword List, Add Keyword in a photo's context menu, upstream #2991) are the fork's; in the Classic shell the Keywording panel is this box and the Keyword List panel the list. Not yet: synonyms in search, person keywords tied to faces, IPTC keywords in exported files (Piwigo, RawTherapee), hierarchy from digiKam / ACDSee / Microsoft XMP fields. In the Keyword List a keyword's empty box carries a dot when the selected photos have a keyword inside it (its tooltip names them), so a folded keyword shows that something in it is applied |

Missing Photos in the sidebar (counted on a worker thread; the view, the Info panel, the grid's context menu and thumbnails read file availability from a cache a worker thread fills, so an offline NAS never stalls a frame); it covers library photos only — Local browse records are never checked, for the count, the view, `library.missing` and Find Missing alike |
| LRC-LIB-CONVERT | Convert to DNG | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:photo.convertToDng`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/convert.rs` | Photo ▸ File ▸ Convert to DNG: lossless DNG next to the raw with the settings embedded, photo (and its virtual copies) relinked; originals kept; undoable; the DNG is decoded and compared with the raw data, written to a temp file, synced, read back and only then given a free name (never replacing a file) — a failed write leaves no DNG and the photo on its raw (issue #106) |
| LRC-LIB-PREVIEWS | Build / discard previews | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:library.buildPreviews`, `cmd:library.previewProgress`, `cmd:library.cancelPreviews`, `cmd:library.clearPreviews`, `cmd:library.discardPreviews`, `cmd:library.previewSettings`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/previews.rs`, `crates/preview/src/lib.rs` | Library ▸ Previews: build standard-sized (Settings → Performance size, or the library's `standardEdge`) or 1:1 previews of the selected / visible photos in the background (progress toasts, stop), Discard 1:1 Previews (selected / visible; standard previews stay), discard the whole cache; 1:1 previews are kept under their own key and discarded automatically after a day / week / month / never (`discardFull`, checked before each build); an import can build standard or 1:1 previews of what it brought in (`atImport`); disk thumbnail + view cache. The zoomed loupe renders its windows itself and does not read 1:1 previews (they serve the quick view); high quality view writes coalesce after 400 ms of quiet; the view preview keeps the full render (a GPU fit at rest reads it back too), a high quality drag's updates cache their drawn size until the next render at rest (PR #506) The loupe shows a kept view render of the same settings that is at least as big as asked for, fitted, with no render (`RenderJob::cached_view`; OneSlips). A build skips photos whose previews are there (`skipped`), writes each to disk before counting it, and starts from `from` in the direction of travel. **Prepare photos in the background** (Settings → Performance, `cmd:prefs.backgroundPrep`, `cmd:prefs.setBackgroundPrep`, `crates/engine/src/prep.rs`): per mode (culling a folder, library, editing) and app-wide or per library, the photos each way of the one in the loupe are rendered ahead (0–20), eye focus strips found ahead, and once the user steps through a few photos the view's previews are built in one cancellable row that waits while the photo on screen renders, pauses for exports and imports, and gives way to a build asked for; photos whose settings changed off screen are rendered again; Which photos: every photo, picks / rated / labelled, or only the photo on screen (OneSlips). |
| LRC-LIB-SLIDESHOW-IMPROMPTU | Impromptu slideshow | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:view.slideshow` | see LR-VIEW-SLIDESHOW |

Each panel is under a foldable header (`classicPanel:<id>`), Solo Mode keeps one open per side, the header's context menu shows/hides panels, toggles Solo Mode and expands/collapses all; order follows `panel.order`; dragging a header up or doThe Navigator is pinned at the top of the column, above the scroll area, as in Classic: the panels below it scroll under it (fork #76); the column ends in Classic's two buttons, Import… / Export… in Library and Copy… / Paste in Develop (`button:leftBar-*`, `bottom_bar`; fork #118). wn its side moves the panel (insertion line, then `panel.order`). Upstream's My Photos sidebar is kept as a panel of Develop's column (`PanelId::Sources`, `sidebarTitle`), hidden until it is asked for and drawn by its own list rather than under a Classic header, so a pull from upstream stays mechanical. The same header menu sits on the Develop tool strip buttons, and offers the side's hiding mode: Manual, Auto Hide (`panel.autoHide`: a click at the window edge brings the hidden side back) or Auto Hide & Show (`panel.autoShow`: resting the pointer there), saved per module. The tool strip is per module (`strip_declared`), and each module's buttons are its own: Develop's are Edit, Crop, Remove, Masking and Red Eye, with the Presets column and More around them, and Library's are its Versions and History & Activity, which open over its Classic right column (a click on the one in use closes it and the column comes back, as the Window menu does); the Library panels Develop's strip used to list (Versions, Activity, Keywords, Info) sat where the user reads Develop's tools (#108) and are not Develop's — Versions and History are in its own left column, and Keywords and Info open from the Window menu and their shortcuts (#45; Library's Classic panels offer no Develop tools, since that click sent the user into another module). Develop's strip is radio-style, as Lightroom Classic's is: clicking the tool in use leaves it on screen; the row under the histogram starts with an Edit button (`icon:toolsEdit`) that closes any tool's drawer, and a dot under a tool marks it applied to the photo (crop, spots, masks, red-eye; #617); closing a tool by its shortcut or the strip's context menu keeps the module in Develop instead of handing the Detail view back to Library. While Crop, Remove, Red Eye or Masking is in use, its drawer takes the Develop panels' place, as in Lightroom Classic: the global panels (Edit, Profile, Basic…) are hidden until the tool is put away (#618; `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/classic_tools.rs`) |

In the window: a mode switcher (Grid · Normal / Live / Locked · Compare · Survey · Slideshow), its own filter bar in Grid (file name, minimum rating, picks only; `second.filter`, applies to its grid, filmstrip and slideshow, not the main window), and a filmstrip under the loupe / compare / survey (`second.filmstrip`); grid tiles and filmstrip thumbnails select the photo (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/second.rs`) |

| LRC-SHELL-IDPLATE | Identity plate (styled text or graphic) and activity centre | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:view.identityPlate`, `cmd:dialog.identityPlateImage`, `cmd:dialog.identityPlateSetup`, `cmd:view.activityList`, `cmd:activity.cancel` | P1.2: the module bar shows the brand mark (drawn in code after `brand/logo.svg`) and the app name, or the user's text, with the status line beside it. Activity centre: while tasks run they take the plate's place, as in Lightroom Classic: one task shows its name, bar and ✕, several "N operations in progress…" over a thin bar each; a click (or `view.activityList`) opens the list of every task with its bar, count, time left and ✕, closed by Esc or a click outside (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/activity.rs`, `docs/background-tasks.md`). Not here: pausing background services (face scan, AI queue) from the list. Graphical plate from an SVG (resvg, pure Rust, no text rendering inside SVGs) or PNG / JPEG file (`image`; `crates/ui-egui/src/plate.rs`), styled text plate (`size`, `color` #rrggbb, `bold`); the plate's context menu picks an image, goes back to text, toggles the mark and bold. Edit ▸ Identity Plate Setup… (issue #121) is Lightroom's Identity Plate Editor: the app's plate or Personalized, styled text (text, size, bold, mark, colour) or a graphic (Choose File… / Clear Image) with a live preview, Show Status and Activity (`showStatus`); edits show in the module bar at once, Cancel restores, the plate is saved with the UI settings. Not here: a font-family choice (the UI font only) and the module picker's typeface |

| LRC-SHELL-PLUGINS | Plug-in manager and SDK (sandboxed) | P2 | 🟡 | `cmd:plugin.list`, `cmd:plugin.install`, `cmd:plugin.grant`, `cmd:plugin.revoke`, `cmd:plugin.enable`, `cmd:plugin.run`, `cmd:plugin.exportDestinations`, `cmd:plugin.exportTo`, `cmd:plugin.metadataFields`, `cmd:plugin.setField`, `cmd:plugin.settings`, `cmd:plugin.setSettings`, `cmd:plugin.prepare`, `crates/plugins/src/manager.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/plugins.rs`, `sdk/examples/export-tools`, `sdk/examples/publish-folder`, `sdk/examples/custom-fields`, `sdk/examples/keyword-picker`, `sdk/examples/program-tools`, `sdk/examples/job-tools`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/plugin_programs.rs`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/plugin_jobs.rs` | P4.3: wasmi sandbox (`crates/plugins`); nothing granted on install: the first call that needs a requested permission (catalog, metadata write, network hosts, fs roots, …) asks the user (Allow runs the command again, Deny is remembered; refused with nobody to ask or in a job), only the user's own input allows, commands can only revoke (`plugin.revoke`), roots over the settings or plug-ins folder are refused, all shown in File ▸ Plug-ins ▸ Plug-in Manager… (Cmd+Alt+Shift+,; a list of plug-ins and pixel filters with generated icons and a page per entry: Settings, Permissions, About with what it adds — commands, hooks, publish service, destinations, fields — and where it comes from (homepage, source with Report a Problem, changelog, licence; `plugins.openLink`), and Log; install from the file picker or a dropped `.wasm`; Show Folder, `plugins.showFolder`); commands with dialogs in File ▸ Plug-ins, which open with their last-run values; a command can prepare its dialog from the selection (its own options, run at once, or a message); plug-in settings on the plug-in's page (Plug-in Manager ▸ Settings tab); export post-process hooks after every export (Export dialog, presets, CLI, control channel, MCP); export destinations (Export dialog ▸ Then send to, `app.export {pluginDestination}`); metadata providers; custom metadata fields in the Metadata panel (active photo, values in the plug-in namespace, read-only fields); publish services (`plugin:<id>` kinds); programs installed on the computer (`programs` permission, allowed by the user like any other; `program.list`, `program.start` with files in a run folder, the `program.end` hook, a row in the activity stack; macOS bundle ids, Windows App Paths / `PATH`, Linux `PATH`); jobs (`job: true` commands, host feature `jobs`): long commands on a worker with a row in the activity stack, progress, detail and photo counts (`job.*` host calls; a batch row with time left and a summary), ✕ / `activity.cancel` / Quit Anyway stop them between calls, `{continue: state}` for work longer than one call's budget, `plugin.run {wait}` for headless sessions, reads only (catalog writes refused in a job); a photo's pixels and its crop (`pixels`: `photo.pixels`, a downsized copy of the uncropped frame; `crop`: `develop.setCrop`, an ordinary crop and straighten angle in that frame, one History step) and virtual copies (`virtualCopy`: `photo.virtualCopy`, stacked with the photo), each a permission and host feature; binary file I/O (base64) and binary HTTP bodies; PhotoCraft-ABI pixel filters (`crates/filters`) at a Source or Display stage of the develop pipeline (`cmd:develop.pluginFilter.add`, `cmd:develop.pluginFilter.analyze`, cached, skipped with a warning when missing); Rust guest SDK (manifest fields filled from the plug-in crate's `Cargo.toml`) + seven examples (`sdk/`) incl. a WebDAV destination, a gallery publish service, job fields, a keyword picker with settings, a program round trip and a job with progress; author guide in docs/plugins.md (G19). Custom fields edit every selected photo (mixed shown), are searchable (filter text, rules `plugin:<id>/<key>`) and round-trip through XMP (`lcpf:fields`, written on save, read back into the plug-in's data on import, Synchronize Folder and Read Metadata from File); plug-in data is kept per library (photo ids are per library) and number fields compare as numbers in rules. Missing: permissions fixed per manifest (no user-entered hosts), no store/signing; after a background export the export hooks and destination run on a worker with an activity row (kind `plugins`, ✕ between plug-ins; catalog requests answered on the UI thread; results in the toast and `export.last.plugins`) |

Photo records stay in memory (compact, shared develop settings): no eviction to the store yet |

| KEYC-DEVELOP | Classic develop keys (V, ⌘U, ⇧⌘U, R, Q, K, M, ⇧M, ⇧W, ⇧J, ⇧Q, ⌥⇧Q…) | P2 | 🟡 | `cmd:develop.treatment`, `cmd:develop.reset`, `cmd:crop.reset`, `cmd:panel.crop`, `cmd:panel.remove`, `cmd:tool.brush`, `cmd:tool.linear`, `cmd:tool.radial`, `cmd:panel.masking` | P1.3 Classic set: R crop, Q remove, K brush, M linear, ⇧M radial, ⇧W masking (Classic's key; the plan said ⇧T, which Classic uses for Guided Upright), ⇧T guided upright; V, ⇧⌘R, ⌥⌘R, W, J, Y, ⇧Y, \ match. Develop module keys: ⌘U Auto, ⇧⌘U Auto white balance, ⌥⇧Q cycles the selected spot Remove → Heal → Clone (`cmd:spot.cycleMode`), Home / End; ⌘/ lists the current module's keys and filters the list to that module. Missing: ⇧J (its Classic meaning not yet verified by observation) |
| KEYC-MODULE-OUTPUT | Book / slideshow / print / map / web keys | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:book.addPage`, `cmd:slideshow.play`, `cmd:slideshow.preview`, `cmd:webui.reload`, `cmd:webui.previewInBrowser`, `crates/ui-egui/src/module.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/web_module.rs` | per-module keymaps (shown by ⌘/): Book ⌘⇧B add page, ⌘Z / ⌘⇧Z layout undo/redo; Slideshow ↩ play, ⌥↩ preview; Print ←/→ page; Map = / − zoom one level (`map.view` `zoomBy`, G28). Web ⌘R reload preview (`webui.reload`, over the global ⌘R Show in Finder while Web is shown), ⌘⌥P preview in browser (`webui.previewInBrowser`: the gallery shown exported to a temporary folder in the background, then its index opened) (G28) |
| KEYC-HELP | Classic help keys (⌘/, F1) | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:module.help`, `cmd:app.shortcuts`, `cmd:app.help`, `crates/ui-egui/src/help_overlay.rs` | F1 help; ⌘/ lays the Classic-style help sheet over the window for the current module (P6.3): the module's own keys (`Module::keymap`) and the global keys that work there, in every module (Library, Develop, Map, Book, Slideshow, Print, Web); any key or click closes it, "All Shortcuts…" opens the editable list (`app.shortcuts`, ⌘/ in the Alternative set), whose "Only <module> keys" filter now knows every module |

### Japanese text watermarks

`app.export` watermark objects accept `vertical` (boolean, defaults to false). The export
dialog offers Horizontal/Vertical and multiline text. Japanese glyphs fall back to the craft-fonts
Mincho faces (BIZ UDMincho; on the web build, BIZ UDPGothic) when built with `CRAFT_FONTS_DIR`. Vertical lettering uses upright em cells and right-to-left newline columns;
Latin stays upright. Advanced Japanese composition remains open. Regression coverage:
`export::tests::japanese_watermarks_support_vertical_columns`, `japanese_watermark_options_and_legacy_defaults`, `watermarks_work_without_craft_fonts`.

## BEYOND. Beyond the reference app

Features with no Lightroom counterpart, tracked like the rest (one row each; `CF-` ids). Wheel semantics read as prose
from Blackmagic Design's *The Colorist Guide to DaVinci Resolve 18* (reference only — no code or assets); the maths
is Chromaforge's own.

| Id | Feature | Tier | Status | Evidence | Missing / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-DUPLICATES | Duplicate Finder: copies across the library, found by content and by picture | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:library.findDuplicates`, `crates/engine/src/duplicates.rs`, `crates/engine/src/phash.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/duplicates_ui.rs`, `crates/engine/src/cmd/duplicates.rs` (tests), `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_duplicates_ui.rs` | Library ▸ Find Duplicates… groups photos that are copies of one picture, the best copy first (most pixels, then the largest file) and the others marked: Remove → Recently Deleted, Reject, Stack Groups. Same-file copies come from the catalog's content hashes at once; Find Similar Copies reads every photo's picture in the background (raw by camera JPEG, image by file, clip by poster) and groups perceptual hashes within 10 of 64 bits (resized, re-compressed or renamed copies), kept on disk in the preview cache; raw + JPEG pairs of the same shot are their own kind, unmarked. Not in the web build (the similar scan reads files); not built: rotation/crop-aware or ML similarity, a Duplicates view, Delete from Disk in the window |
| CF-GRADE-MODE | Grading modes: Eboda and Witchcraft | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:view.gradingMode`, `cmd:section.primaries`, `cmd:section.hsl`, `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/witchcraft.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_grading_mode.rs`, `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_witchcraft.rs` | Settings ▸ General ▸ Grading Mode (beside the Layout row; also Window ▸ Grading Mode, Ctrl+Shift+G; app-level like the Compact layout) picks **Witchcraft** (the Resolve-style layout below — the default) or **Eboda** (the Lightroom Classic panels). One recipe, two presentations: presets, history, XMP and scopes are shared. Witchcraft's Edit panel runs **Basic, then Primaries, then HSL Curves** (the grade continues where every photo starts), the two colourist sections carrying a small **Witchcraft pill** beside their titles (`sectionBadge:primaries`/`hsl`; gone in Eboda with their sections), and the Modern layout's Colour ▸ Color Grading card follows the mode (the Primaries palette and HSL curves in Witchcraft, Lightroom's 3-way wheels in Eboda). The histogram above keeps its own mode menu (its scopes included). Follow-ups, not built: parallel/layer mixers, shared nodes, gallery/stills à la PowerGrades, Primaries Bars |
| CF-GRADE-PRIMARIES | Colourist primaries: the Color wheels and the Log wheels, both at once | P2 | ✅ | `ctl:primaries.*`, `crates/pipeline/src/colorops.rs` (`PrimariesK`, `log_zones`), `crates/gpu/src/wgsl/finish.wgsl` (`primaries`), `crates/gpu/tests/it/equivalence.rs` | Two sets of wheels (hue/saturation puck, luminance master wheel), **both always rendering** — complimentary as Resolve's Primaries palette, where the Color wheels (Lift / Gamma / Gain / Offset) establish the range and the Log wheels (Shadow / Midtone / Highlight / Offset, `primaries.logWheels.*`, narrow ranges between `primaries.low`/`high`) refine it; the palette's Color \| Log tabs only pick which set is edited. All with the Color Grading wheels' drag holds (Shift keeps hue, Cmd/Ctrl saturation, Alt fine, double-click reset). The Color set is the published **ASC CDL SOP** per channel on display-linear values after the tone map — lift's affine `x·(1−l)+l` pivots at white, gain's multiply at black, gamma a power (exponent 1/(1+p), clamped 0.25..4), offset adds last; the Log set refines additively over its zones (shadow/midtone tilt, highlight scales, log offset moves everything), each wheel at its own tuned strength. Neutral wheels skip the per-pixel maths and are not written to settings (JSON and `hash64` stable; an earlier experimental `log` switch key is left unread). CPU and GPU equivalence-tested (each set and both); copy/paste/preset group and history group ("grading") included; `lc:settings` XMP only (no `crs:` counterpart). Follow-ups, not built: Primaries Bars view, HDR zone wheels |
| CF-VIGNETTING-CALIBRATION | Vignetting Calibration: non-destructive flat-field correction from reference raws (as Capture One's LCC "Uniform Light") | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:vignettingCalibration.create`, `cmd:vignettingCalibration.apply`, `cmd:vignettingCalibration.remove`, `cmd:vignettingCalibration.withdraw`, `cmd:vignettingCalibration.references`, `ctl:optics.vignettingCalibrationAmount`, `crates/raw/src/vignetting_calibration.rs`, `crates/raw/src/vignetting_plausibility.rs`, `crates/pipeline/src/vignetting_calibration.rs`, `crates/catalog/src/vignetting_maps.rs`, `crates/engine/src/vignetting_calibration_library.rs`, `crates/engine/src/tests_vignetting_calibration_commands.rs`, `crates/gpu/tests/it/equivalence.rs` | Develop ▸ Lens Corrections ▸ Vignetting Calibration: Create measures a reference raw (checked first: a photo that isn't a flat field is refused), Apply gives each photo the reference for its camera model, lens, aperture and zoom focal length, Amount 0–120 %, part of the develop settings (undo, History, Sync / Copy / Paste, presets, sidecars); one reference in use per combination and image area, a full-frame one serving crop modes; references found by a Library filter, a smart-collection rule and a grid marker. Luminance only (no colour cast); no interpolation between apertures; no in-body-stabilisation shift. Library ▸ Flat-Field Correction… (#1079) is the separate tool that writes corrected DNG copies |
| CF-GRADE-HSL-CURVES | HSL curves: free points over the hue circle | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:develop.hslCurves`, `cmd:develop.hslPick`, `crates/pipeline/src/colorops.rs` (`HslK`, `hsl_curve_at`), `crates/gpu/src/wgsl/finish.wgsl` (`F_HSL`), `crates/ui-egui/src/panels/witchcraft.rs` (`hsl_curves`), `crates/ui-egui/src/tests_witchcraft.rs` | Hue vs Hue / Hue vs Sat / Hue vs Lum ("the first word prompts the selection, and the second word affects the change") as free points anywhere over the hue circle — not fixed colour bands: a click plants a point, a drag moves it (one undo step a drag), a double-click or right-click ▸ Delete Control Point removes it (the Tone Curve's own `POINT_GAP` and `with_point` shared, so points keep apart), and a **colour picker** beside the tabs plants the picked photo hue as a point at the curve's current height (`develop.hslPick`; the hue read as the curves receive it). The monotone spline through the points (neutral at the hue axis's ends) is what renders — 128-entry LUTs sampled identically by the CPU loop and the GPU kernel, applied with the mixer's own scales (hue ±0.5 rad, saturation ×(1±1), luminance ±0.18; hue and luminance fade towards neutrals). The x axis is the OkLCh hue circle, shown by the hue strip under the plot; points clamp to x 0..1, y −1..1 and cap at `MAX_CURVE_POINTS`. Colour photos only (B&W sits out, as the mixer); copy/paste with the Color Mixer group, history under "color". Follow-ups, not built: wrapping the curve at the red seam, Sat vs Sat, Lum vs Sat |

## IMM. Immich integration

Beyond Lightroom: the catalog and a self-hosted [Immich](https://immich.app) server as one library (PLAN.md §2.14).
We only talk to Immich over its HTTP API with our own client; concepts, endpoints and the supported versions are in
`plan/immich.md`. The local test server is `cargo xtask immich up|seed|down` (`xtask/immich/compose.yml`).

| Id | Feature | Tier | Status | Evidence | Missing / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IMM-CONNECT | Connect to one or more Immich servers (URL + API key), version check, secure key storage | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.connect` `cmd:immich.test` `cmd:immich.disconnect` `cmd:immich.status`, `crates/immich`, Settings → Connections (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/connections.rs`); version ≥ 3.0 check, permissions (the card lists only what the key lacks; the full list behind Show permissions), TOFU fingerprint. Keys in the Secret Service keychain (locked keyring: Unlock Keychain prompt, `cmd:credentials.unlockSystem`) or a passphrase-encrypted file unlocked once per session (`cmd:credentials.unlock` `cmd:credentials.useStore` `cmd:credentials.status`), used automatically where no keychain is reachable (macOS/Windows). Connect and Check run on workers (`background`). Native macOS Keychain / Windows Credential Manager backends are still missing (FFI); the encrypted file stands in. Checked against the v3.3.1 test server (headless snapshots). See [immich.md](immich.md) | Phase 1 |
| IMM-LINK | Link catalog photos to Immich assets by checksum; "in Immich" badge and filter | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.link` `cmd:immich.confirmLink` `cmd:immich.unlink` `cmd:immich.links`, `cmd:remote.pump` (SHA-1 back-fill); SHA-1 else probable by name + time + size; grid badge, filter `immich`, Info "Open in Immich", smart-album field `immich` | Phase 1 |
| IMM-IMPORT | Immich as an import source (browse albums/timeline, download originals or add as linked) | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.import` `cmd:immich.browse` `cmd:immich.fetchOriginal`, `cmd:file.importImmich` opens the Import dialog on its Immich source (Files ↔ Immich switch); copy / link only (original on first Develop; kind and format follow the original, `Op::SetKind`), one-way metadata, duplicates skipped by checksum | Phase 1 |
| IMM-EXTLIB | Shared-originals mode: Immich external library and catalog over the same folders, no duplicate uploads | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.libraries` `cmd:immich.setPathMaps` `cmd:immich.writeSidecars` `cmd:immich.pushSidecars` `cmd:immich.scanLibraries`, `crates/immich/src/extlib.rs`: coverage table, path mapping (suggested by folder name); external-library assets linked by mapped path (Immich doesn't hash them); XMP for mapped folders then sidecar discovery + refresh-metadata. Verified against a v3.3.1 external library scan (ignored live test `live_external_library_links_by_path_and_reads_sidecars`) | Phase 1 |
| IMM-SIDECARS | XMP sidecars both ways: pull the sidecar Immich stores for a linked asset, push the catalog's sidecars and metadata | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.pullSidecars` `cmd:immich.pushSidecars`, `crates/immich/src/sidecars.rs`, `crates/immich/src/client.rs` (`asset_files`, `asset_sidecar`, `download_asset_file`; `GET /asset-files`, v3.2.0), `crates/engine/src/cmd/immich_sidecars.rs`, Settings → Connections → XMP Sidecars (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/connections.rs`): pull applies the stored XMP (metadata + `lc:settings` develop settings) as one undo step and writes the local sidecar, skipping a photo edited after the asset unless `overwrite`; push writes whole XMP sidecars for external-library folders and sends the fields Immich keeps (rating, description, date, location, tags) for managed assets, which Immich merges into the sidecar it holds; `full` reads the sidecar Immich holds, merges the catalog's packet into it and copies it back through a tiny temporary asset (`PUT /assets/copy`), then removes it (the original is never deleted or re-uploaded). Version-gated on v3.2.0; verified against v3.3.1 (ignored live test `live_asset_sidecar_round_trip`) | Phase 4 |
| IMM-SHARELINK | Create Immich shared links for published albums (from Web/Slideshow/Publish) | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:web.shareImmich`, `cmd:webui.shareImmich`, `crates/immich/src/share.rs` | P3.8: Web module ▸ Share via Immich: renders the gallery's large images, uploads them into a new Immich album and creates a shared link with expiry, password, allow-download and show-metadata options; Copy URL. From Slideshow / Publish: not yet |
| IMM-PUBLISH | Immich publish service: collections → albums, renders and/or originals, re-publish, stacks | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:publish.createService` (`kind: immich`, `settings: {account, send: rendered\|original\|both, deleteRemoved}`), `cmd:publish.run`, `crates/immich/src/publish.rs`: collection = Immich album (found by name or created), SHA-1 pre-check (`bulk-upload-check`, no duplicates), linked originals never re-uploaded (shared originals), both = render stacked on the original, re-publish uploads the new render and trashes the old one, removal takes it out of the album (trash only with `deleteRemoved`). Engine-side opener in `crates/engine/src/cmd/immich_publish.rs`. Album remembered by id: renaming the collection or moving it into another collection set renames the same Immich album on the next run; collection sets below the service set are name prefixes (`Set / Collection`, `publish.createCollection {parent}`); uploads retried with backoff, each attempt checksum-checked first (no duplicates when an answer is lost), interrupted runs resume by checksum; Publish Services Immich form (account, send, trash removed renders). an uploaded original carries the photo's whole XMP sidecar (IMM-SIDECARS). Live test `live_publish_renames_its_album`; version matrix (v3.0.0 and `release`) in `.github/workflows/immich.yml` via `cargo xtask immich up --version`. Rename happens on the next publish run, not at rename time | Phase 4 |
| IMM-SYNC | Two-way metadata sync (rating, favourite, title/description, tags ↔ keywords, albums ↔ collections, GPS, time, archive) | P1 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.sync` `cmd:immich.syncStatus` `cmd:immich.syncConflicts` `cmd:immich.resolveConflict` `cmd:immich.setSync` `cmd:immich.resolveDeletion` `cmd:immich.linkAlbum` `cmd:immich.syncAlbums` `cmd:immich.resolveAlbumDeletion`, `crates/immich/src/sync.rs`: field-wise three-way merge against the last synced values, per-field direction and policy (newest wins by default; GPS/date ask), keywords merged as sets (export-excluded never sent), conflicts kept with both values, deletions queued (never automatic, trash only), dry run, activity log, background interval with backoff, offline-safe (nothing changes, cursor kept). Albums ↔ collections (`crates/immich/src/albums.rs`): a per-server album rule (direction + policy, like the field rows): off = only albums linked by hand; to Immich / from Immich / two-way = every plain album maps to the Immich album of the same name with creates, renames, membership and album deletes (never photo deletes; deletes follow the policy, or wait for the user when the policy is ask or the other side changed too); published collections, smart albums and the Quick Collection stay out. Album and background metadata runs hold an activity row; Sync Now and the schedule run the album rule too. UI: Settings → Connections → Sync… (rules incl. albums, schedule, conflicts, deletion queues, one latest-run status line). Polls `updatedAfter` (not `/sync/stream`); scale (100k) untested | Phase 4 |
| IMM-PEOPLE | Import Immich people and face regions into People; push names back | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.importPeople` `cmd:immich.confirmFaces` `cmd:immich.pushPeople`, `crates/immich/src/people.rs`: faces → face regions marked from Immich (`immich:<person>`), hidden people skipped, overlapping regions kept, confirmed ones survive re-import; names back as renames, shared names as merges (opt-in). Birth dates are reported, not stored | Phase 4 |
| IMM-SEARCH | Immich smart (CLIP) and metadata search from the Library filter bar | P2 | ✅ | `cmd:immich.smartSearch` (`mode: smart\|ocr\|description\|place`, `show` → temporary collection via the filter's photo list, `saveAs` → album); filter bar "Immich smart search" box (`crates/ui-egui/src/panels/connections.rs` `smart_search_box`, runs on a worker) | Phase 4 |
| IMM-MCP | Immich commands available via CLI, control channel and MCP | P2 | ✅ | every Immich action is an `immich.*` command (`cmd:immich.sync`, `cmd:immich.smartSearch`, …), dispatched alike by UI, CLI, control channel and MCP | Phase 4 |
